REQUEST: send Elif Farah a note
TOOLS:
T0 (F11:ID:user F9:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T1 (F8:ID:card F0:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T2 (F8:ID:card F4:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T3 (F8:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T5 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T6 (F8:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T7 (F8:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T8 (F11:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
FIELDS:
F0 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F1 card STR :: card.title
F2 user STR :: user.email
F3 card TIME :: card.created
F4 card STR :: card.status
F5 user STR :: user.name
F6 card BOOL :: card.archived
F7 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F8 card ID:card :: card.id
F9 - STR :: message text
F10 card TIME :: card.due
F11 user ID:user :: user.id
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:user :: Elif Farah's user id
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T0 C0 C1
STOP

REQUEST: find any non-urgent unarchived cards, report back to me, then message the assignee of each of them
TOOLS:
T0 (F10:ID:card F5:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T1 (F10:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T2 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T3 (F10:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T4 (F10:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T5 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T6 (F7:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T7 (F10:ID:card F3:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T8 (F7:ID:user F1:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
FIELDS:
F0 user STR :: user.name
F1 - STR :: message text
F2 card BOOL :: card.archived
F3 card STR :: card.status
F4 user STR :: user.email
F5 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F6 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F7 user ID:user :: user.id
F8 card TIME :: card.due
F9 card STR :: card.title
F10 card ID:card :: card.id
F11 card TIME :: card.created
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: false
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T5 -> r0
FILTER r0 F6 EQ C0 AND F2 EQ C0 -> r1
PAUSE

REQUEST: fetch the "Audit permissions" card and let the assignee of it know; if that fails, let Uma Quist know
TOOLS:
T0 (F0:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T1 (F0:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T2 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T3 (F9:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T4 (F9:ID:user F2:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T5 (F0:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T6 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T7 (F0:ID:card F6:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T8 (F0:ID:card F4:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
FIELDS:
F0 card ID:card :: card.id
F1 card STR :: card.title
F2 - STR :: message text
F3 user STR :: user.email
F4 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F5 card BOOL :: card.archived
F6 card STR :: card.status
F7 card TIME :: card.due
F8 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F9 user ID:user :: user.id
F10 card TIME :: card.created
F11 user STR :: user.name
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:card :: Audit permissions
C1 STATUS :: the OK status
C2 STR :: message text
C3 ID:user :: Uma Quist's user id
C4 STR :: failure notice text
PROGRAM:
TRY -> r0
  CALL T1 C0 -> r1
IF r0 EQ C1
  CALL T4 r1.F4 C2
ELSE
  CALL T4 C3 C4
STOP

REQUEST: For every in good standing customer, purge each of their tickets from Cyberdyne.
TOOLS:
T0 (F11:ID:ticket) -> OBJ:ticket [WRITE] :: Mark a support ticket as closed.
T1 () -> LIST OBJ:customer [READ] :: List every customer account.
T2 (F2:ID:customer) -> OBJ:customer [READ] :: Fetch one customer account by id.
T3 () -> LIST OBJ:ticket [READ] :: List every support ticket, open and closed.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List the staff users (account managers and billing).
T5 (F2:ID:customer F10:BOOL) -> OBJ:customer [WRITE] :: Flag or unflag a customer account as delinquent.
T6 (F20:STR F9:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send an email to an address.
T7 () -> LIST OBJ:invoice [READ] :: List every invoice.
T8 (F14:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch one staff user by id.
T9 (F11:ID:ticket) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a support ticket.
T10 (F7:ID:invoice) -> - [SEND] :: Send an invoice to its customer for payment.
FIELDS:
F0 ticket ID:customer :: ticket.customer
F1 user STR :: user.name
F2 customer ID:customer :: customer.id
F3 customer ID:user :: customer.manager
F4 ticket STR :: ticket.status
F5 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F6 ticket TIME :: ticket.opened
F7 invoice ID:invoice :: invoice.id
F8 invoice BOOL :: invoice.paid
F9 - STR :: email body
F10 customer BOOL :: customer.delinquent
F11 ticket ID:ticket :: ticket.id
F12 customer STR :: customer.name
F13 invoice ID:customer :: invoice.customer
F14 user ID:user :: user.id
F15 customer STR :: customer.plan
F16 user STR :: user.email
F17 invoice INT :: invoice.amount
F18 customer STR :: customer.email
F19 customer TIME :: customer.signup
F20 - STR :: recipient email address
F21 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: false
C1 ID:customer :: Cyberdyne's customer id
PROGRAM:
CALL T1 -> r0
CALL T3 -> r1
FILTER r0 F10 EQ C0 -> r2
FOREACH r2 -> r3
  FILTER r1 F0 EQ r3.F2 AND F0 EQ C1 -> r4
  FOREACH r4 -> r5
    CALL T9 r5
STOP

REQUEST: notify the user with the most archived cards
TOOLS:
T0 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T1 (F3:ID:card F11:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T2 (F3:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T3 (F3:ID:card F4:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T5 (F3:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T6 (F6:ID:user F0:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T7 (F3:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T8 (F6:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
FIELDS:
F0 - STR :: message text
F1 card STR :: card.title
F2 user STR :: user.email
F3 card ID:card :: card.id
F4 card STR :: card.status
F5 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F6 user ID:user :: user.id
F7 card BOOL :: card.archived
F8 user STR :: user.name
F9 card TIME :: card.created
F10 card TIME :: card.due
F11 card ID:user :: card.assignee
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: true
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T4 -> r0
CALL T0 -> r1
FILTER r1 F7 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F11 EQ r3.F6 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F11 EQ r6.F6 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T6 r3 C1
STOP

REQUEST: Go ahead and move the "Plan offsite" card into progress, and try again a couple of times if it errors out.
TOOLS:
T0 (F11:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T1 (F0:ID:user F10:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T2 (F11:ID:card F9:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T3 (F11:ID:card F3:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T4 (F11:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T5 (F11:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T6 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T7 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T8 (F0:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
FIELDS:
F0 user ID:user :: user.id
F1 card BOOL :: card.archived
F2 user STR :: user.email
F3 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F4 card STR :: card.title
F5 card TIME :: card.due
F6 card TIME :: card.created
F7 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F8 user STR :: user.name
F9 card STR :: card.status
F10 - STR :: message text
F11 card ID:card :: card.id
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:card :: Plan offsite
C1 STR :: the doing status
PROGRAM:
TRY RETRY 3 -> r0
  CALL T2 C0 C1 -> r1
STOP

REQUEST: For every customer, purge each of their closed tickets.
TOOLS:
T0 (F17:ID:customer F18:BOOL) -> OBJ:customer [WRITE] :: Flag or unflag a customer account as delinquent.
T1 () -> LIST OBJ:ticket [READ] :: List every support ticket, open and closed.
T2 (F4:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch one staff user by id.
T3 () -> LIST OBJ:invoice [READ] :: List every invoice.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List the staff users (account managers and billing).
T5 (F6:ID:ticket) -> OBJ:ticket [WRITE] :: Mark a support ticket as closed.
T6 (F8:STR F13:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send an email to an address.
T7 (F17:ID:customer) -> OBJ:customer [READ] :: Fetch one customer account by id.
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:customer [READ] :: List every customer account.
T9 (F5:ID:invoice) -> - [SEND] :: Send an invoice to its customer for payment.
T10 (F6:ID:ticket) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a support ticket.
FIELDS:
F0 customer STR :: customer.name
F1 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F2 ticket ID:customer :: ticket.customer
F3 user STR :: user.email
F4 user ID:user :: user.id
F5 invoice ID:invoice :: invoice.id
F6 ticket ID:ticket :: ticket.id
F7 customer TIME :: customer.signup
F8 - STR :: recipient email address
F9 user STR :: user.name
F10 ticket STR :: ticket.status
F11 customer STR :: customer.email
F12 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
F13 - STR :: email body
F14 invoice INT :: invoice.amount
F15 customer ID:user :: customer.manager
F16 invoice BOOL :: invoice.paid
F17 customer ID:customer :: customer.id
F18 customer BOOL :: customer.delinquent
F19 invoice ID:customer :: invoice.customer
F20 ticket TIME :: ticket.opened
F21 customer STR :: customer.plan
CONSTANTS:
C0 STR :: the closed status
PROGRAM:
CALL T8 -> r0
CALL T1 -> r1
LET r0 -> r2
FOREACH r2 -> r3
  FILTER r1 F2 EQ r3.F17 AND F10 EQ C0 -> r4
  FOREACH r4 -> r5
    CALL T10 r5
STOP

REQUEST: Hey — message the user with the most overdue cards, thanks!
TOOLS:
T0 (F0:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T1 (F7:ID:card F10:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T2 (F7:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T3 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T4 (F7:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T5 (F7:ID:card F4:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T6 (F0:ID:user F11:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T7 (F7:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
FIELDS:
F0 user ID:user :: user.id
F1 user STR :: user.email
F2 card BOOL :: card.archived
F3 user STR :: user.name
F4 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F5 card TIME :: card.created
F6 card STR :: card.title
F7 card ID:card :: card.id
F8 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F9 card TIME :: card.due
F10 card STR :: card.status
F11 - STR :: message text
CONSTANTS:
C0 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T8 -> r0
CALL T3 -> r1
FILTER r1 F9 LT NOW -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F4 EQ r3.F0 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F4 EQ r6.F0 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T6 r3 C0
STOP

REQUEST: I need you to message the user with the most archived cards.
TOOLS:
T0 (F4:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T1 (F4:ID:card F3:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T2 (F6:ID:user F0:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T3 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T4 (F6:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T5 (F4:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T6 (F4:ID:card F5:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T7 (F4:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
FIELDS:
F0 - STR :: message text
F1 user STR :: user.name
F2 card BOOL :: card.archived
F3 card STR :: card.status
F4 card ID:card :: card.id
F5 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F6 user ID:user :: user.id
F7 card STR :: card.title
F8 card TIME :: card.due
F9 card TIME :: card.created
F10 user STR :: user.email
F11 card BOOL :: card.urgent
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: true
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T8 -> r0
CALL T3 -> r1
FILTER r1 F2 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F5 EQ r3.F6 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F5 EQ r6.F6 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T2 r3 C1
STOP

REQUEST: Could you send out every unpaid invoices again.
TOOLS:
T0 (F14:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch one staff user by id.
T1 (F18:ID:customer F7:BOOL) -> OBJ:customer [WRITE] :: Flag or unflag a customer account as delinquent.
T2 () -> LIST OBJ:ticket [READ] :: List every support ticket, open and closed.
T3 (F15:ID:ticket) -> OBJ:ticket [WRITE] :: Mark a support ticket as closed.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List the staff users (account managers and billing).
T5 (F16:STR F4:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send an email to an address.
T6 (F15:ID:ticket) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a support ticket.
T7 (F1:ID:invoice) -> - [SEND] :: Send an invoice to its customer for payment.
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:invoice [READ] :: List every invoice.
T9 (F18:ID:customer) -> OBJ:customer [READ] :: Fetch one customer account by id.
T10 () -> LIST OBJ:customer [READ] :: List every customer account.
FIELDS:
F0 customer ID:user :: customer.manager
F1 invoice ID:invoice :: invoice.id
F2 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F3 ticket ID:customer :: ticket.customer
F4 - STR :: email body
F5 invoice BOOL :: invoice.paid
F6 customer STR :: customer.plan
F7 customer BOOL :: customer.delinquent
F8 ticket TIME :: ticket.opened
F9 invoice ID:customer :: invoice.customer
F10 customer STR :: customer.name
F11 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
F12 user STR :: user.name
F13 customer STR :: customer.email
F14 user ID:user :: user.id
F15 ticket ID:ticket :: ticket.id
F16 - STR :: recipient email address
F17 user STR :: user.email
F18 customer ID:customer :: customer.id
F19 invoice INT :: invoice.amount
F20 customer TIME :: customer.signup
F21 ticket STR :: ticket.status
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: false
PROGRAM:
CALL T8 -> r0
FILTER r0 F5 EQ C0 -> r1
FOREACH r1 -> r2
  CALL T7 r2
STOP

REQUEST: For every user, set each of their cards assigned to June Kerr to done
TOOLS:
T0 (F9:ID:card F11:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T1 (F0:ID:user F2:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T2 (F9:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T3 (F9:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T5 (F0:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T6 (F9:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T7 (F9:ID:card F5:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
FIELDS:
F0 user ID:user :: user.id
F1 user STR :: user.name
F2 - STR :: message text
F3 user STR :: user.email
F4 card STR :: card.title
F5 card STR :: card.status
F6 card TIME :: card.due
F7 card BOOL :: card.archived
F8 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F9 card ID:card :: card.id
F10 card TIME :: card.created
F11 card ID:user :: card.assignee
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:user :: June Kerr's user id
C1 STR :: the done status
PROGRAM:
CALL T4 -> r0
CALL T8 -> r1
LET r0 -> r2
FOREACH r2 -> r3
  FILTER r1 F11 EQ r3.F0 AND F11 EQ C0 -> r4
  FOREACH r4 -> r5
    CALL T7 r5 C1 -> r6
STOP

FIELDS:
F0 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
F1 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F2 ticket STR :: ticket.status
F3 customer BOOL :: customer.delinquent
F4 ticket TIME :: ticket.opened
F5 customer STR :: customer.email
F6 user STR :: user.email

PROGRAM:

  CALL T8 C0 -> r1

  CALL T5 r1.F5 C2

  CALL T5 C3 C4
STOP

STOP

T1 (F5:ID:card F11:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T2 (F8:ID:user F4:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T3 (F5:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T5 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T6 (F5:ID:card F7:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T7 (F8:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T8 (F5:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
FIELDS:
F0 card TIME :: card.created
F1 user STR :: user.name
F2 card BOOL :: card.archived
F3 user STR :: user.email
F4 - STR :: message text
F5 card ID:card :: card.id
F6 card STR :: card.title
F7 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F8 user ID:user :: user.id
F9 card TIME :: card.due
F10 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F11 card STR :: card.status
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:user :: Vik Reyes's user id
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T5 -> r0
CALL T4 -> r1
FILTER r1 F7 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F7 EQ r3.F8 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F7 EQ r6.F8 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T2 r3 C1
STOP

T6 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T7 (F1:ID:card F9:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T8 (F1:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.

F1 card ID:card :: card.id
F2 card STR :: card.title
F3 - STR :: message text
F4 card TIME :: card.created
F5 user ID:user :: user.id
F6 user STR :: user.name
F7 card TIME :: card.due
F8 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F9 card STR :: card.status
F10 user STR :: user.email
F11 card BOOL :: card.urgent
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:card :: Ship mobile beta
C1 STATUS :: the OK status
C2 STR :: message text
C3 ID:user :: Ada Gray's user id
C4 STR :: failure notice text
PROGRAM:

  CALL T3 r1.F8 C2

  CALL T3 C3 C4
STOP

T3 (F4:ID:card F2:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T4 (F4:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T5 (F4:ID:card F11:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T6 (F0:ID:user F6:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T7 (F0:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T8 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
FIELDS:
F0 user ID:user :: user.id
F1 user STR :: user.name
F2 card STR :: card.status
F3 user STR :: user.email
F4 card ID:card :: card.id
F5 card BOOL :: card.archived
F6 - STR :: message text
F7 card TIME :: card.created
F8 card STR :: card.title
F9 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F10 card TIME :: card.due
F11 card ID:user :: card.assignee
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:user :: June Gray's user id
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T1 -> r0
CALL T8 -> r1
FILTER r1 F11 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F11 EQ r3.F0 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5

  FILTER r2 F11 EQ r6.F0 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T6 r3 C1
STOP

STOP

TOOLS:
T0 (F4:ID:card F1:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.

T3 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T4 (F10:ID:user F3:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T5 (F4:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T6 (F10:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T7 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T8 (F4:ID:card F8:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
FIELDS:
F0 user STR :: user.name
F1 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F2 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F3 - STR :: message text
F4 card ID:card :: card.id
F5 user STR :: user.email
F6 card BOOL :: card.archived
F7 card STR :: card.title
F8 card STR :: card.status
F9 card TIME :: card.due
F10 user ID:user :: user.id
F11 card TIME :: card.created
CONSTANTS:
C0 STR :: the todo status
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:

    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3

T3 (F2:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
T4 (F6:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T5 (F6:ID:user F7:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T6 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T7 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T8 (F2:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
FIELDS:
F0 user STR :: user.name
F1 card BOOL :: card.archived
F2 card ID:card :: card.id
F3 card STR :: card.title
F4 user STR :: user.email
F5 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F6 user ID:user :: user.id

F8 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F9 card TIME :: card.created
F10 card TIME :: card.due
F11 card STR :: card.status

C0 ID:user :: Ivan Reyes's user id
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T6 -> r0
CALL T7 -> r1

FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F8 EQ r3.F6 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5

  FILTER r2 F8 EQ r6.F6 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T5 r3 C1
STOP

TOOLS:

T5 (F3:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T6 (F3:ID:card F6:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T7 (F9:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.

FIELDS:
F0 card BOOL :: card.urgent
F1 card STR :: card.status
F2 user STR :: user.name
F3 card ID:card :: card.id
F4 user STR :: user.email
F5 card STR :: card.title
F6 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F7 card TIME :: card.created
F8 card TIME :: card.due
F9 user ID:user :: user.id
F10 card BOOL :: card.archived
F11 - STR :: message text
CONSTANTS:
C0 STR :: the todo status
PROGRAM:

  FILTER r1 F6 EQ r3.F9 AND F1 EQ C0 -> r4
  FOREACH r4 -> r5
    CALL T5 r5 -> r6
STOP

REQUEST: Can you message the user with the most unarchived cards?
TOOLS:
T0 (F4:ID:card F3:ID:user) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Assign a card to a user.
T1 (F4:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [READ] :: Fetch a single card by its id.
T2 (F8:ID:user F0:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send a direct message to a user.
T3 (F8:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch a single user by id.
T4 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List every user on the board.
T5 (F4:ID:card) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Archive a card so it no longer shows on the board.
T6 () -> LIST OBJ:card [READ] :: List every card on the board, archived ones included.
T7 (F4:ID:card F6:STR) -> OBJ:card [WRITE] :: Set a card's workflow status (todo, doing, or done).
T8 (F4:ID:card) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a card. This cannot be undone.
FIELDS:
F0 - STR :: message text
F1 user STR :: user.email
F2 user STR :: user.name
F3 card ID:user :: card.assignee
F4 card ID:card :: card.id
F5 card STR :: card.title
F6 card STR :: card.status
F7 card BOOL :: card.archived
F8 user ID:user :: user.id
F9 card TIME :: card.created
F10 card TIME :: card.due
F11 card BOOL :: card.urgent
CONSTANTS:
C0 BOOL :: false
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T4 -> r0
CALL T6 -> r1
FILTER r1 F7 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F3 EQ r3.F8 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F3 EQ r6.F8 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T2 r3 C1
STOP

STOP

T7 (F20:ID:customer F17:BOOL) -> OBJ:customer [WRITE] :: Flag or unflag a customer account as delinquent.
T8 (F4:STR F8:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send an email to an address.
T9 (F9:ID:ticket) -> OBJ:ticket [WRITE] :: Mark a support ticket as closed.
T10 (F19:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch one staff user by id.
FIELDS:
F0 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
F1 invoice INT :: invoice.amount
F2 ticket ID:customer :: ticket.customer
F3 customer STR :: customer.email
F4 - STR :: recipient email address
F5 customer TIME :: customer.signup
F6 customer STR :: customer.plan
F7 invoice BOOL :: invoice.paid
F8 - STR :: email body

F10 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F11 user STR :: user.email

F13 customer STR :: customer.name

F20 customer ID:customer :: customer.id
F21 invoice ID:customer :: invoice.customer

C0 ID:customer :: Wonka Ops's customer id
C1 STR :: message text
PROGRAM:
CALL T3 -> r0
CALL T2 -> r1
FILTER r1 F2 EQ C0 -> r2
FIRST r0 -> r3
FILTER r2 F2 EQ r3.F20 -> r4
COUNT r4 -> r5
FOREACH r0 -> r6
  FILTER r2 F2 EQ r6.F20 -> r7
  COUNT r7 -> r8
  IF r8 GT r5
    LET r8 -> r5
    LET r6 -> r3
CALL T8 r3.F3 C1
STOP

  FOREACH r4 -> r5
    CALL T4 r5 C0 -> r6
STOP

REQUEST: Could you fetch Stark Labs and send a notice to it; if that fails, tell the billing desk.
TOOLS:
T0 () -> LIST OBJ:ticket [READ] :: List every support ticket, open and closed.

T3 () -> LIST OBJ:user [READ] :: List the staff users (account managers and billing).
T4 (F9:ID:invoice) -> - [SEND] :: Send an invoice to its customer for payment.
T5 (F8:ID:ticket) -> - [DELETE] :: Permanently delete a support ticket.
T6 (F4:ID:customer) -> OBJ:customer [READ] :: Fetch one customer account by id.
T7 (F0:STR F18:STR) -> - [SEND] :: Send an email to an address.
T8 (F19:ID:user) -> OBJ:user [READ] :: Fetch one staff user by id.
T9 (F8:ID:ticket) -> OBJ:ticket [WRITE] :: Mark a support ticket as closed.
T10 () -> LIST OBJ:invoice [READ] :: List every invoice.
FIELDS:
F0 - STR :: recipient email address
F1 customer STR :: customer.plan
F2 customer ID:user :: customer.manager
F3 customer STR :: customer.name
F4 customer ID:customer :: customer.id
F5 invoice TIME :: invoice.due
F6 ticket TIME :: ticket.opened
F7 customer TIME :: customer.signup
F8 ticket ID:ticket :: ticket.id
F9 invoice ID:invoice :: invoice.id
F10 ticket STR :: ticket.status
F11 user STR :: user.name
F12 invoice INT :: invoice.amount
F13 invoice ID:customer :: invoice.customer
F14 customer STR :: customer.email
F15 invoice BOOL :: invoice.paid
F16 user STR :: user.email
F17 customer BOOL :: customer.delinquent
F18 - STR :: email body
F19 user ID:user :: user.id
F20 ticket ID:customer :: ticket.customer
F21 ticket INT :: ticket.priority
CONSTANTS:
C0 ID:customer :: Stark Labs
C1 STATUS :: the OK status
C2 STR :: message text
C3 STR :: the billing desk address
C4 STR :: failure notice text
PROGRAM:
TRY -> r0
  CALL T6 C0 -> r1
IF r0 EQ C1
  CALL T7 r1.F14 C2
ELSE
  CALL T7 C3 C4
STOP
